營收走勢圖

研究員/ 鄭高輯 owencheng@uni-psg.com

結論: 
維持聯電買進建議，理由包括: (1) 2Q26 毛利率及 EPS 表現優於
預期。(2) 終端市場不佳的背景下，3Q26 毛利率及稼動率表現仍
強勁，市佔率持續提升。(3)AI 已經貢獻今年 3 億美元營收，未來
三年超過 10 億美元。(4) 12 吋 PIC 已開始量產，TFLN 調變器也
將是首家量產的公司。(5) 先進封裝包括 DTC 中介層、3D 堆疊，
將於 2026 年~2027 年初開始貢獻。(6) 上調 2026 年資本支出至
20 億美元，擴充矽光子及先進封裝產能。預估2026、2027 年稅後
EPS 分別為 7.27 元(業外貢獻 2.5 元)及 7.33 元，考量價格環境
改善 ， 且矽光及先進封裝為新動能 ， 給予PER 20X， 目標價146 元，
維持買進建議。

重點摘要: 
1.2Q26 毛利率及 EPS 表現優於預期:  
聯電 2Q26 營收 12.6%QoQ(+17.0%YoY)，主要係受惠於通訊及消費
性電子的強勁需求 ， 出貨量+10.6%QoQ 至 112.9 萬片 ， 優於財測的
+高個位數 QoQ 成長；ASP 提升低個位數%，符合財測。在毛利率方
面，較前季提升3.3pt 至 32.5%，優於財測的「接近30%」，主要
係 UTR 由 79%提升至 85%，優於財測的 81~83%。業外認列 302.36
億 ， 主要係投資收益 。 單季稅後EPS 3.39 元 ， 大幅優於預期的1.38
元。本季22/28nm 需求持續強勁，比重由34%提升至 37%，再創新
高。

2.終端市場不佳的背景下，3Q26 毛利率及稼動率表現仍強勁:  
展望 3Q26，PC、通訊與消費性電子需求持續穩健，且 8 吋受惠於
PMIC、MCU 強勁需求，帶動UTR 大幅提升。在財測方面，預估出貨
量成長高個位數%QoQ，ASP 維持穩健，毛利率提升至 34~36%，產
能利用率進一步提升至 90%以上。

儘管 AI 需求強勁，但非 AI 領域復甦並非全面復甦，公司觀察智
慧型手機和消費的庫存和 DOI 同步上升，消費支出疲軟讓短期手
機及 PC 出貨 YoY 下滑，另汽車工業領域的需求雖穩定，但DOI 仍
高於歷史平均，因此將謹慎觀察庫存變化。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

儘管如此 ， 聯電特殊製程佔比達50%以上 ， 且公司持續在晶圓代工
增加市佔率，同時客戶在 AI 和非 AI 市場市佔率也在增加，因此
公司預計第三季整體 UTR 將大於 90%，12 吋高於公司平均，而 8
吋 UTR 也有 85%。

3.上調資本支出至 20 億美元，擴充矽光子及先進封裝產能:  
公司將 2026 年資本支出由 15 億美元上調至 20 億美元，主要用於
新加坡 12i P4 及台南 12A P7/8 擴產。其中，新加坡 P4 廠無塵
室是為了矽光子產能而準備；台南新建的晶圓廠是為先進封裝而
準備，由於建設時間拉長，預估會有大於20 個月的準備時間，因
此量產預計到 2028~2029 年。此外，預算增加有一部份為新加坡
P3 產能擴充，將增加 BCD 以及矽光子產能，另台南也將投入客製
化記憶體堆疊(CMS)和 DTC，同時在 8 吋建立薄膜鈮酸鋰(TFLN)產
能。此外，董事會會議已核准將近50 億美元資本支出，用於未來
2~3 年，同時折舊曲線將改變，預估 2026、2027 年折舊金額增速
將會是每年增加低十位數。

4.AI 已經貢獻今年 3 億美元營收，未來三年超過 10 億美元:  
AI 相關已開始成為公司 2026 年成長的重要動能，相關產品包括: 
PMIC、通訊(connectivity)、FPGA、先進封裝和矽光子。公司預估
2026 年AI 營收將近 3 億美元(預估佔比約 3%)，未來三年預計將
超過 10 億美元(預估佔比約高個位數%)。

5. 先進封裝包括 DTC 中介層、3D 堆疊，將於 2026 年~2027 年初
開始貢獻:  
在先進封裝方面， 公司預期到2030 年可觸及的市場將成長一倍以
上。目前技術已擴展到獨立深槽電容(DTC)的 2.5D 中介層、3D 晶
圓對晶圓堆疊(RF SOI) 以及記憶體對記憶體堆疊技術。目前已有
10 多個活躍客戶 ， 並有超過35 個新產品正在討論中 ， 預計在2026
年~2027 年初開始貢獻。

6. 12 吋 PIC 已開始量產，TFLN 調變器也將是首家量產的公司 
公司本月已將首批 12 吋矽光子 PIC 量產晶圓交付客戶，2027 年
將進一步推出可供更多客戶導入之矽光子平台，12 吋 PIC 提供更
好的製程控制和效能，跟競爭對手 8 吋做出區隔。至於薄膜鈮酸
鋰(TFLN)， 公司已經將是全球上第一個將TFLN 調變器投入生產的
公司，這是傳輸速度超過 400G 的最佳解決方案。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

7.2027 年價格有機會更好，且光通及先進封裝為新動能，預估稅
後 EPS 7.33 元:  
展望 2027 年，就價格而言，公司認為受惠AI 和非 AI 相關需求推
動 ， 訂價環境變得更具有支撐 ， 預計今年訂價趨勢會比預期好 ， 並
預期在 2027 年會有更有意義的價格上漲 。 此外 ，AI 相關應用已經
成為公司新的成長動能，光通相關的 PIC 及 TFLN，以及先進封裝
(DTC 中介層、3D 堆疊)等逐步放量，加上公司配合客戶產能擴充，
可望延續 2026 年成長重要成長動能。研究員預估 2027 年營收
+22.8%YoY，稅後 EPS 7.33 元(原估 5.87 元)。

基本面需求依舊強勁，BB ratio 顯著攀升至 2.2 
 短期股價大幅回檔，已修正 PER，基本面環境仍屬正向：投顧預估
2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 20.17 元及 34.1 元，目前股價 502
元，PER 25X(2026EPS)及 15X(2027EPS)不高。短期稼動率拉升至
90%，BB ratio 上看 2.2 的水準，均有利後續的營收及毛利率表現，
由於 AI 伺服器之 MLCC 用量為通用伺服器的 10~15 倍 ， 加上HVDC
增加耗用量的時間點自 2027 年逐步開始，市場預期 2028 年獲利更
上看 50~60 元，長線仍可預期股價仍有上看 1,500 元以上的機會，
短期股價大幅回檔，已顯著修正 PER，基本面環境仍屬正向，故給
予投資建議買進，給予目標價 1,200 元。 
 展望 3Q26，需求強勁，BB 值顯著攀升，稼動率上看 9 成，營收、
毛利率、營益率持續揚升，單季稅後EPS 5.4 元：展望 3Q26，2H26
需求展望，進入 2H26 市場需求維持非常強勁的態勢；強烈的正面市
場信號，2Q26 BB ratio 逐月改善，至 6 月底大幅攀升至 2.2；新專
案投資信心 ， 特定關鍵客戶對其新專案的投資信心充足， 並要求國巨
在技術工程及產能上提供全面支持；長期協議 (LTA)簽署意願高，客
戶為保障供應鏈安全 ， 簽署長期合約的意願顯著增加 ， 甚至願意支付
溢價以鎖定產能 ； 產能利用率目標 ， 為因應強勁需求並建立健康的庫
存 ， 國巨計劃在3Q26 將高階特殊品與標準品的產能利用率均調升至
90%以上(2Q26 標準品利用率約為 80%)；價格與利潤方面，2Q26
已進行價格調整以轉嫁材料成本上漲 ， 若後續材料成本持續增加 ， 公
司不排除進一步調整價格；3Q26 財測指引，預估營收將呈現溫和成
長，毛利率與營業利益率預期將較 2Q26 微幅增加，3Q26 的營運動
能來自較高的產能利用率及出貨量帶來實質貢獻 ， 惟受惠2Q26 進行
的定價策略及產品組合優化，預期 3Q26 ASP 將會更高，定價效果
會更為顯著。投顧維持預估 3Q26 營收 483 億元、 +9%QoQ、
+46%YoY，單季稅後 EPS 5.4 元(原 5.9 元)。

國巨*係全球電子元件領導者 ，
提 供 業 界 領 先 的 電 子 元 件 產

國巨*係全球電子元件領導者 ，提供業界領先的電子元件產品 ，提供全球客戶「一
次購足服務」 
國巨是全球電子元件領導者，為多元產業提供可靠且創新的解決方案。結合國巨
(YAGEO) 、 基 美(KEMET) 、普思 (PULSE) 及 特 勒 美 科 感 測 器(Telemecanique 
Sensors)的品牌優勢，提供業界領先的電子元件產品，推動 AI、汽車、航太、電信
與工業等領域的進步 。 國巨提供全球客戶 「一次購足服務」 ， 供應完整的電子元件，
包括電阻、電容、電感、變壓器、繼電器、天線、無線元件及電路保護元件。同時
提供上述電子元件的 OEM 與 ODM 客製化設計與製造服務，滿足客戶在各種不同
領域的應用需求。國巨擁有超過 100 個全球據點，結合美洲、歐洲、中東、非洲及
亞洲在地支援，實現全球佈局與本地服務的完美結合。旗下品牌憑藉基美的研發實
力、普思的連接技術創新，以及國巨的規模製造等優勢，確保能有效滿足各地市場
需求並交付優質元件。國巨 2025 年營收 1,329 億元、+9%YoY，毛利率 36.21%、
+1.85pptsYoY，營益率 22.42%、+3.2pptsYoY，業外淨收入 13.18 億元，稅後淨
利 236 億元、+22%YoY，稅後 EPS 11.41 元。

2Q26 營收季成長廣泛分佈局於
各產品線、業務部門、區域及通

2Q26 營收季成長廣泛分佈局於各產品線、業務部門、區域及通路。AI 與標準、特
製產品皆持續成為推動成長的核心關鍵驅動力 
2Q26 營收依產品比重，磁性元件 (Magnetics)占 24%、+9%QoQ、+19%YoY；
Tantalum 占 24%、+14%QoQ、+47%YoY；MLCC 占 19%、+17%QoQ、+35%YoY；
Resistor 占 14%、+23%QoQ、+40%YoY；Sensor 占 11%、-2%QoQ、+62%YoY；
Others 占 8%、+96%QoQ、+24%YoY。強勁的 AI 內容需求，持續推動多項產品
類別的成長 ，Tantalum、MLCC、Resistor 均有雙位數季成長 ， 其中MLCC、Resistor
受惠標準及特製產品的強勁需求，季成長率優於平均水準。 
根據通路商 Future electronics 七月的 Market Conditions Repor t 資料上顯示，國
巨的 Magnetics、MLCC 及 Resistor 產品交期均顯著拉長，且價格趨勢由上季持平
轉為進入上漲趨勢中。 
2Q26 營收依終端應用，以美元金額來看，所有業務部門皆實現季成長， AI 持續成
為正向驅動力，目前占營收比重約 16%，推升運算與企業來到 27%水準。其中工
業占 28%、+14%QoQ、+31%YoY， 已連續六個季度實現成長 ； 運算與企業占27%、
+28%QoQ、+40%YoY，其中 PC 市場疲軟，造成了不利影響；汽車占 16%、
+8%QoQ、+12%YoY， 維持正向的發展動能 ； 消費占12%、+9%QoQ、+43%YoY，
需求依然強勁；電信占 12%、+21%QoQ、+67%YoY；航太/國防/醫療占 4%、
+8%QoQ、+64%YoY。工業、運算&企業及電信均有雙位數季成長，運算&企業及
電信季成長均優於平均水準。

品的強勁需求所致。

銷商為最，均有雙位數季成長，客戶端對於 2H26 的市場情緒及營運展望普遍持正
面、樂觀態度。

展望 3Q26，需求強勁，BB 值顯著攀升，稼動率上看 9 成，營收、毛利率、營益
率持續揚升，單季稅後 EPS 5.4 元 
展望 3Q26，2H26 需求展望，進入 2H26 市場需求維持非常強勁的態勢；強烈的正
面市場信號 ，2Q26 BB ratio 逐月改善 ， 至6 月底大幅攀升至 2.2； 新專案投資信心，
特定關鍵客戶對其新專案的投資信心充足，並要求國巨在技術工程及產能上提供全
面支持；長期協議(LTA)簽署意願高，客戶為保障供應鏈安全，簽署長期合約的意
願顯著增加，甚至願意支付溢價以鎖定產能；產能利用率目標，為因應強勁需求並
建立健康的庫存，國巨計劃在 3Q26 將高階特殊品與標準品的產能利用率均調升至
90%以上(2Q26 標準品利用率約為 80%)；價格與利潤方面，2Q26 已進行價格調整
以轉嫁材料成本上漲，若後續材料成本持續增加，公司不排除進一步調整價格；
3Q26 財測指引，預估營收將呈現溫和成長，毛利率與營業利益率預期將較 2Q26
微幅增加，3Q26 的營運動能來自較高的產能利用率及出貨量帶來實質貢獻，惟受
惠 2Q26 進行的定價策略及產品組合優化，預期 3Q26 ASP 將會更高，定價效果會
更為顯著。投顧維持預估 3Q26 營收 483 億元、+9%QoQ、+46%YoY，毛利率
40.14%、+1.64ppts QoQ 、+3.95pptsYoY，營益率 28.74%、+2.31pptsQoQ、
+5.88pptsYoY，稅後淨利 111 億元、+19%QoQ、+76%YoY，單季稅後 EPS 5.4
元(原 5.9 元)。

展望 2026 年，除了 PC 相對疲
弱外，餘應用面表現相對強勁，
投顧預估 營收年增 38%，稅後
EPS 34.1 元

展望 2026 年，除了 PC 相對疲弱外，餘應用面表現相對強勁，投顧預估營收年增
38%，稅後 EPS 34.1 元 
展望 2026 年，目前應用面以 NB、消費性電子、智慧型手機需求相對偏弱，但 PC
並未嚴重惡化或急劇收縮，惟相較對他板塊偏弱，如 3Q26 季增維持持平。而 AI、
工業板塊表現相對強勁，工業已連續多季成長，且歐洲市場展現強勁復甦，航太、
國防、醫療亦持續維持強勁。投顧預估 2026 年營收 1841 億元、YoY 38% ，毛利
率 39.73%、YoY 3.52ppts，營益率 28.04%、+5.62pptsYoY，稅後淨利 417 億元、
+77%YoY，稅後 EPS 20.17 元。同時預估 2027 年營收 2,565 億元、+39%YoY，

本次報告：法說會

國巨* (2327 TT) 
營運逐季向上趨勢明確

永豐觀點 
B/B Ratio 逐月往上，2026/6 月 B/B Ratio 為 2.2，且越來越多客戶願意溢價確保
供貨順暢。另一方面，AI 佔比也提升至 16%。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：主係看好國巨營運將受惠於 (1) 日韓廠商將低階 MLCC 產能
移轉至高階產品的訂單外溢效應，(2) 管理層預估 3Q26 稼動率提升至 90%。另
一方面，下半年漲價效應浮現將進一步推升毛利率表現，(3) 除 MLCC 外，鉭質電
容、電感及晶片電阻的用量增加。綜上所述，考量國巨受惠轉單效應、被動元件產
業循環向上、仍有持續漲價機會，以 2027-2028 年平均 EPS 預估，目標價 1240
元(30 X 2027-28F 平均 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
國巨企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

### Page 2

2

國巨集團為台灣被動元件第一大供應商：2Q26 營收產品別占比 MLCC 19.1%，
Resistor 14.3%，T antalum(Kemet) 23.8%，Magnetics 23.5%，Sensor 10.9%，
Other Components 8.4%，其中 MLCC 與電阻成長主要來自標準品與特殊品需求
強勁 ； 終端應用占比 ：Industrial 28.4%，Computing & Enterprise System 27.3%，
Automotive 15.9%，Consumer 12.2%，T elecom 12.2%，Aerospace/Defense 
/Medical 4%，其中 AI 佔比約為 16%，較上季成長一個百分點。 
產品漲價及稼動率提升 ，2Q26 毛利率 38.5%：國巨於第二季針對部分產品價格調
漲 20-40%不等，除因原物料價格上升外，因 AI 伺服器使部分高階產品需求明顯
提升。整體而言，所有產品線、區域及終端應用領域皆為成長。國巨 2Q26 營收
444.56 億元 (+16.5% QoQ ， +35.7% YoY) ， 在 稼 動 率 提 升 下 ， 毛 利 率
38.5%(+0.4ppt QoQ，+2.9ppt YoY)， 營利率26.4%(+1.2pptQoQ，+4.9pptYoY)，
稅後淨利 94.18 億元(+17.7%QoQ，+88.4%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 4.59 元，因業外
認列未分配盈餘稅，影響 EPS 約-0.22 元，使 EPS 不及預期的 4.9 元。 
日韓產能溢出，國巨將直接受惠：雖相較於日韓國際大廠，國巨高階 MLCC 產品
市占率約為 10-20%，然因 Nvidia 下一世代 Vera Rubin 平台 MLCC 用量將較
Blackwell 平台近翻倍成長，且高階 MLCC 產品所耗用產能為中低階 MLCC 產品
的 8-10 倍。此外，因日韓廠商高階MLCC 產能滿載，故預期國巨將受惠於日韓廠
商將低階 MLCC 產能移轉至高階產品的訂單外溢效應，使得國巨在中低階 MLCC
訂價權將有更大的市場主導能力，營運將明顯受惠此效應。 
B/B Ratio 高達 2.2，3Q26 營運樂觀：展望第三季，受惠需求持續增加，除 B/B 
Ratio 不斷上升外，越來越多客戶對 LTA表現興趣，願支付溢價確保供應無虞。另
一方面，3Q26 稼動率持續提升，標準品及高階特殊品皆將提升至90%，上季分別
為 80-85%及 85-90%。在受惠於稼動率持續提升及漲價效應顯現下，預估國巨
3Q26 營收 490.7 億元(+10.4% QoQ，+48.3% YoY)， 毛利率40%(+1.5ppt QoQ，
+3.8ppt YoY)，營利率 27.2%(+0.8pptQoQ，+4.3pptYoY)，稅後淨利 112.45 億
元(+19.4%QoQ，+76.9%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 5.48 元。 
2Q26 各通路經銷商庫存健康：因預期未來市場在伺服器及車用需求增加，供需缺
口擴大使得產品將漲價， 供應商或通路商將提前進貨並控制庫存水位； 觀察國巨在
2017-19 年存貨狀況，國巨在 4Q17 起存貨明顯增加並延續 5 季至 1Q19 存貨開
始下降，營收高點在 3Q18，存貨高點則於 4Q18。觀察目前狀況，2Q26 無論在
大中華及全球通路商，和高服務(線上)通路皆較去年同期降低。 
產業循環向上空間可期：在 AI 伺服器帶動下，被動元件用量明顯增加，進一步帶
動日韓廠商發出漲價通知，雖國巨未能直接受惠於高階 MLCC 產品用量的增加，
然因轉單效應的顯現，仍使國巨中低階產品具有市場主導權。此外，國巨近年併購
轉型有成，產品多元，產業應用廣泛，獲利趨於穩定成長。目前以 AI 相關終端需
求最為強勁，國巨產品亦將受惠鉭質電容、電感及晶片電阻的用量增加。2026 年
AI 需求強勁，稼動率及產品價格已於 2Q26 提升，未來仍有上修空間，預估國巨
2026 年營收成長至 1850.6 億（+39.2%YoY） ，毛利率39.7%(+3.5pts YoY)，營利
率 27.1%(+4.7pts YoY)， 稅後淨利415.5 億元(+75.8%YoY)， 稅後EPS 為 20.23 元。 
展望未來，因產能擴張約需 1-1.5 年，產能缺口難在短期補足，轉單效應將至少延
續至 2Q27，被動元件產業循環仍在持續向上，預期2027 年 MLCC 仍有 30%以上
的漲價空間，其餘被動元件也有望因產能排擠使供給出現缺口帶動下波漲價，故預
估國巨 2027 年營收 2638.2 億（+42.6%YoY） ，毛利率46.1%(+6.4pts YoY)，營利
率 33.4%(+6.3pts YoY)，稅後淨利733.2 億元(+76.5%YoY)，稅後EPS 為 35.4 元。

聯發科 (2454  TT)  Trading Buy

ASIC 業 務 帶 動 營 運 與 評 價 ，投 資 建 議 給 Trading Buy 。  
投 資 建 議 ： 聯發科對 2026 年 手 機 業 務 看 法 保 守 ， 但 2026~2028
年的 ASIC 業 務 看 法 正 向 ，投 資 建 議 給 Trading Buy 。  
聯 發 科 2Q26 獲 利 季 增 、 年 減： 2Q26 使 用 的 匯 率 為 31.6 ， 聯 發
科 2Q26 營收 1,521.83 億元， QoQ+2.03% \YoY+1.21% ； 稅 後 純
益 243.35 億 元 ，QoQ+0.75% \YoY -12.62% ； 稅 後 EPS 15. 17 元。 
聯 發 科 3Q26 估 營 收 約 季 持 平：以匯率 32 元 計 算，3Q26 營 收 區
間為 1,522~1,598 億元， QoQ+0~5% \YoY+7~12% ，毛利率
46± 1.5% 、 費 用 率 31± 2.0% 。 3Q26 智 慧 裝 置 平 台 業 務 的 成 長 ，
將 抵 銷 手 機 業 務 下 滑 所 帶 來 的 影 響 。 
聯 發 科 目 標 全 年 美 元 營 收 YoY 高 個 位 數 成 長 ， 聯 發 科 預 計 漲 價 ，
全 年 毛 利 率 目 標 與 2Q26 相當。  
AI ASIC 業 務 展 望 更 樂 觀： 聯發科第一個 AI 加 速 器 ASIC 將於
4Q26 開 始 量 產 ， 預 計 2026 全 年 資 料 中 心 將 貢 獻 超 過 20 億美
元 ， 並 且 在 2027 年 倍 數 成 長 ， 聯 發 科 預 計 AI ASIC 在 2027 年
的 市 場 規 模 將 達 到 800 億 美 元 ， 聯 發 科 目 標 市 佔 率 從10~15% ，
上修至 15~20% 。  
第二個 AI 加 速 器 ASIC 將於 2028 年 初 量 產 ， 聯 發 科 有 信 心 在
2028 年 的 市 占 率 將 提 升 。 
群 益 預 估 聯 發 科 2026 年 \2027 年稅後 EPS 60. 78 元 \76.07 元 ：
群 益 預 估 聯 發 科 2026 年 \2027 年 營 收 為 6,138.45 億元 \10 ,435.36
億 元 ，YoY+3.00% \+70.00% ； 稅 後 純 益 974.78 億元 \1,220.04 億
元， YoY -7.4 5%\+25.16% ；稅後 EPS 60. 78 元 \76.07 元。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
環 境 保 護：聯 發 科 技 做 為 全 球 半 導 體 產 業 技 術 領 導 者，在 整 體 價 值 鏈 中 位 於
最 前 端 的 晶 片 設 計，後 端 的 原 物 料 採 購、代 工 製 造、封 裝 與 測 試 等 環 節 皆 交
由 供 應 商 執 行，形 成 專 業 分 工 與 互 相 合 作 的 夥 伴 關 係。根 據 風 險 調 查 與 碳 足
跡 盤 查 發 現，主 要 對 社 會 與 環 境 產 生 衝 擊 影 響 的 關 鍵 在 於 後 端 製 造 作 業，故
如 何 與 供 應 商 共 同 合 作，以 降 低 社 會 與 環 境 相 關 的 潛 在 風 險，為 聯 發 科 技 發
展 綠 色 營 運 的 首 要 任 務 。 
社 會 責 任：運 用 數 位 科 技 解 決 社 會 問 題 不 是 企 業 的 專 利，聯 發 科 技 相 信 每 個
人 都 有 潛 力 利 用 數 位 科 技 創 造 無 限 可 能，聯 發 科 發 起 數 位 社 會 創 新 競 賽 「 智
在 家 鄉 」 ， 鼓 勵 社 會 大 眾 為 家 鄉 問 題 提 供 解 方 。 
公 司 治 理：聯 發 科 技 董 事 會 下 設 有 審 計 委 員 會，強 化 公 司 治 理 獨 立 性，並 設
立 薪 資 報 酬 委 員 會 ， 以 健 全 公 司 薪 酬 制 度 為 目 標 ， 持 續 強 化 企 業 永 續 發 展 。
除 前 述 法 定 功 能 性 委 員 會 外，本 公 司 亦 設 立 法 定 以 外 之 併 購 策 略 委 員 會，視
實 際 需 求 針 對 集 團 具 體 併 購 案 件 及 整 體 併 購 策 略 評 估 分 析 。 
產 品 組 合 ：  
2Q26 產 品 組 合 ： 手 機 晶 片 業 務 41% 、 智 慧 裝 置 平 台 業 務 53% 、 PMIC 6% 。  
手 機 業 務 全 年 展 望 保 守：  
2Q26 手 機 晶 片 業 務 營 收 QoQ -14% \YoY -20% ， 營 收 占 比 41% ， 衰 退 原 因 是
手機的 BOM 表 的 成 本 上 漲 ， 聯 發 科 預 計 2026 年 全 球 手 機 出 貨 量 YoY 約 -
15% 。  
客 戶 重 視 旗 艦 級 產 品 與 價 格 敏 感 的 入 門 級 產 品，聯 發 科3Q26 將 推 出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ， 為 下 一 代 AI 模 型 提 供 動 力 ， 聯 發 科 與 全 球 廠 商 合 作 ， 持 續 擴 大
2027 年 旗 艦 級 手 機 的 市 占 率 。 
供 應 鏈 成 本 上 漲 已 經 是 產 業 現 象，聯 發 科 將 對 價 格 進 行 調 整，以 確 保 成 本 上
漲 可 以 反 映 在 產 品 定 價 中 。3Q26 手 機 業 務 展 望 季 持 平 或 季 減 中 個 位 數 百 分
比 ， 旗 艦 級 SoC 的 量 產 可 以 抵 消 大 部 分 其 他 手 機 業 務 的 衰 退 。 
客 戶 重 視 旗 艦 級 產 品 與 價 格 敏 感 的 入 門 級 產 品，聯 發 科3Q26 將 推 出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ， 為 下 一 代 AI 模 型 提 供 動 力 ， 聯 發 科 與 全 球 廠 商 合 作 ， 持 續 擴 大
2027 年 旗 艦 級 手 機 的 市 占 率 。 
智 慧 邊 緣 平 台 業 務展 望 正 向 ：  
2Q26 智 慧 邊 緣 平 台 業 務 營 收 QoQ+19 \YoY+26% ， 營 收 占 比 53% 。 成 長 動 能
包 含 在 運 算 、 車 用 的 市 佔 率 的 提 升 ， 以 及TV SoC 的 成 長 。 多 項 連 接 、 車 用
項 目 走 向 量 產 ， 聯 發 科 預 計 此 業 務 將 季 增 中 高 個 位 數 營 收。  
2Q26 PMIC 營 收 占 比 約 6% ：  
2Q26 PMIC 業 務 營 收 QoQ+11% \YoY+6% ， 營 收 占 比 6% 。 成 長 動 能 包 含 資
料 中 心 的 市 占 率 提 升 ， 聯 發 科 預 計 此 業 務 將 季 持 平 。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 3 日

3 群益投顧 
AI ASIC 業 務 展 望 更 樂 觀：  
聯 發 科 第 一 個 AI 加 速 器 ASIC 將於 4Q26 開 始 量 產 ， 預 計 2026 全 年 資 料 中
心 將 貢 獻 超 過 20 億 美 元，並 且 在 2027 年 倍 數 成 長，聯 發 科 預 計 AI ASIC 在
2027 年 的 市 場 規 模 將 達 到 800 億 美 元，聯 發 科 目 標 市 佔 率 從 10~15% ，上 修
至 15~20% 。 第 二 個 AI 加 速 器 ASIC 將於 2028 年 初 量 產 ， 聯 發 科 有 信 心 在
2028 年 的 市 占 率 將 提 升 。 
聯 發 科 認 為 每 代 的 ASIC 的 毛 利 率 相 當 ， 並 不 會 更 高 ，ASIC 的 毛 利 率 低 於
目 前 公 司 平 均 ， 但 新 世 代 的 ASIC 對 營 收 的 貢 獻 貴 更 大 。 
其 他 補 充 ：  
聯發科 448G SerDes 的 研 發 順 利 ， 採 用 CPO 的 解 決 方 案 。448G SerDes 之
後 ， 聯 發 科 會 在 台 積 電(2330 TT) 的 COUPE 平 台 上 ， 繼 續 發 展 CPO 的 解 決
方 案 。 聯 發 科 還 有 提 供 3.5D 的 平 台 ， 該 平 台 提 供 IP 、 封 裝 與 設 計 流 程 ， 適
用 於 大 型 資 料 中 心 的 設 計 。 
供 應 鏈 成 本 上 漲 已 經 是 產 業 現 象，聯 發 科 將 對 價 格 進 行 調 整，以 確 保 成 本 上
漲 可 以 反 映 在 產 品 定 價 中 。 
聯 發 科 目 前 還 在 投 入 新 技 術，絕 對 費 用 金 額 還 會 再 增 加，但 是 由 於 營 收 規 模
上 升 ， 費 用 率 會 下 降 。 
Forward PE Band

資料來源：CMoney，群益預估彙整。

7 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

Yageo (2327.TW)
2Q26 Beat on Broad-Based Strength; Recalibrating TP as Sector 
Multiple Resets, Structural Thesis Intact
CITI'S TAKE 
Yageo’s 2Q26 results beat across scale and margins, with AI revenue mix 
rising to 16% and BB ratio climbing to 2.2, underscoring accelerating 
forward demand. Mgmt. ’s 3Q26E guidance for moderate rev growth and 
further margin improvement, alongside commodity/specialty UTR set to 
reach 90%+, points to a tightening S/D backdrop, while rising customer 
interest in multi-year LTAs across AI, computing, and memory sectors 
signals extending demand visibility. We raise our 2026E-28E EPS forecasts 
by 11%-13% and lift our ASP growth CAGR assumptions. That said, we 
adjust our TP to NT$1,450 (from NT$1,500), applying a 35x average 2027-
28E EPS (vs. 40x prior) to reflect the sector’s ongoing, deleveraging-driven 
multiple compression, while maintaining our positive view and Buy rating.
2Q26 results beat on scale and margins — Yageo’s reported 2Q26 net profit at 
NT$9.4bn (+18% QoQ/+88% YoY) beat Citi/cons forecasts by 15%/3%, driven by 
better scale, margin expansion, and opex control. GPM came in at 38.5% (+0.4ppt 
QoQ/+2.9ppts YoY), below cons estimate as higher commodity shipments diluted 
mix; however, we are starting to see early pricing pass-through flow into margins. AI 
revenue mix rose to 16% (from c.14-15% in 1Q26), while the BB ratio climbed MoM to 
2.2 by end-Jun (vs. 1.3 at AGM), pointing to robust forward demand visibility.
Positive from all leading indicators — Mgmt. guided 3Q26 revenue to moderate 
growth with GPM/OPM improving slightly, as commodity and specialty UTR are set 
to rise from 2Q26’s 80%/85% to 90%+/90%+, and mgmt. does not rule out further 
price hikes if raw material costs keep climbing. Mgmt. further flagged rising 
customer interest in signing LTAs across AI, computing, and memory end-markets, 
implying underlying demand is running ahead of prior expectations. We accordingly 
raise our 2025-28E ASP growth CAGR assumptions to 22%/23%/27% (from 
20%/16%/25%) across Yageo’s MLCC/resistor/tantalum lines.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 11%-13% to reflect 
stronger results and guidance. We adjust our TP to NT$1,450, implying 35x avg. 
2027E-28E EPS (from NT$1,500 based on 40x), a moderate step-down reflecting 
the sector’s deleverage-driven multiple compression rather than any change to 
Yageo’s earnings trajectory. We expect profitability and growth visibility to keep 
improving through the cycle. Reiterate Buy.

Bull/Bear: Yageo (2327.TW)

### Page 8

Yageo (2327.TW)
30 July 2026 Citi Research
8
Yageo
Company description
Established in 1977 and listed in 1993, Yageo is a leading supplier of passive
electronic components based in Taiwan. The company offers a wide array of
products including MLCC, chip resistors, magnetics, tantalum, and other
components, aiming to be the total solution provider for its customers. By
capacity, the company currently ranks No. 1 in chip resistor and tantalum and
No. 3 in MLCC and inductor globally. Key customers of Yageo include leading

EMS, ODMs, and distributors. The company also has 29 sales offices, 51
manufacturing sites, and 20 R&D centers in 29 nations worldwide.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Yageo shares. Yageo is widely known as a passive
electronic component supplier. Although passive components are generally
viewed as a commodity, Yageo is no longer a commodity play, in our view,
given the company’s foray into premium markets through M&As, which
should help mitigate the impact of sector cyclicality and order volatility. We

expect a more diversified product portfolio to drive stable earnings and better
margins, while improving utilization in its commodity component business
following the end of inventory digestions should add additional fuel.
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E
earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the company’s earnings
performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market.
Our target multiple of 35x is at the higher end of the trading range since 2022.

During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and
started to deliver stable margins and earnings.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-
expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and pricing competition; 3)
execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and
interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

## Document 074: 260729_daiwa_unimicron

Source PDF: all report pdf/260729_daiwa_unimicron.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-07-29
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our latest view on Unimicron after 2Q26 results.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat. Unimicron’s 2Q26 earnings came 
in at TWD13.1bn (EPS: TWD8.53), which was 196% and 141% higher than 
our and the street’s expectations due to a higher-than-expected gross 
margin and non-operating gains. Unimicron’s 2Q26 revenue grew 15% 
QoQ to TWD42.9bn, while we attribute revenue strength to price hikes on 
substrate and new capacity ramp. Management attributes half of the 
margin expansion in 2Q26 to price hikes and the rest to utilisation rate 
(UTR) and yield improvement. As such, gross margin expanded 6.9pp QoQ 
to 24.8%; operating margin thus expanded by 8.2pp QoQ on gross margin 
expansion and better opex control. Additionally, Unimicron recognised a 
mark-to-market gain of TWD9.2bn mainly from its holding in UMC in 2Q26.

Business outlook. We believe management holds a positive view on both 
revenue and gross margin trends in 2H26 given the rising AI substrate and 
PCB sales mix and ongoing cost inflation. In the results call, we noted 
investors are keen to know the development of EMIB-T and glass-core 
substrate, capacity expansion plan, margin trend/pricing trend, HDI 
strategy, etc. For EMIB-T, management guides that end customers will 
likely adopt this technology for at least three generations in their products, 
which would lead to Unimicron and the other two JP substrate companies 
investing in related equipment in this new packaging technology and target 
the first stage of yield rate at 50% in 2H27E. Of total capex, management 
believes only 1-2% is tailored for EMIB-T equipment, while most of the rest 
(c.85%) is still marked for ABF substrate capacity expansion. As Unimicron 
further raised capex guidance to TWD53.7bn, we expect its ABF capacity 
expansion plan to reach 40%/20%/20% in 2026/27/28E respectively. 
Unimicron may also drive slightly incremental capacity from de-
bottlenecking. As for HDI strategy, it mentioned no direct contest with its 
Chinese competitor and a focus on non-China markets. Considering the 
enlarging substate body size due to die size or co-packaging requirements 
and advanced technology has to cooperate with substrate, mgmt. expects 
value from substrate to gradually increase with long-term GM target at 50-
60%. As such, we expect GM to expand to 26.3/38.6/45.4% (up 3-7pp vs. 
our prior estimate) in 2026/27/28, respectively, along with price hike trend.

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 31-66% due to higher 
gross margin assumptions. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M 
TP to TWD1,500 on an unchanged target PER of 35x applied to 1-year-
forward EPS. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected PC/server demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 26-33% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish gross margin assumptions.

29 July 2026

2Q26 results: on-track gross margin expansion

➢ Gross margin further expands on price hikes and technology migration 
➢ Strong capex guidance indicates a decent ABF substrate outlook  
➢ Reiterating Buy (1) call; lifting 12M TP to TWD1,500 (from TWD1,000)

Company profile 
Unimicron is a leading global manufacturer of IC substrate. The company also produces high-density 
interconnection (HDI) boards, flexible PCBs (FPC), and rigid flex PCBs (RFPCB). The company has 
manufacturing sites in Taiwan, China, Japan and Germany, with products' major applications including 
servers, PC/NB, and smartphones.

Bull/Bear: Chipbond Technology (6147.TWO)

### Page 6

Chipbond Technology (6147.TWO)
31 July 2026 Citi Research
6
Chipbond Technology
Company description
Chipbond, established in 1997, provides turnkey services for back-end
assembly processing of LCD driver ICs, from wafer bumping to packaging.
Chipbond is the largest LCD driver IC back-end supplier.
Investment strategy
We rate Chipbond shares as Buy, as we see signs of stabilization in its main
DDIC business. Despite tepid smartphone demand, OLED would partially

support its ASP of its testing business with penetration on the rise steadily.
Meanwhile, the company would potentially gain share in DDIC from Korean
packaging peers as well. The company would also raise the price to customers
to reflect the inflation. Further, the company is developing optical products
with its gold bumping technology. Given its leading position in gold bumping,
we believe Chipbond would benefit from it meaningfully in the next few years.

Valuation
Our target price for Chipbond of NT$230 is derived from a DCF model. We use
a 1-year forward-looking DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect
Chipbond’s ability to generate stable cash. Using a risk-free rate of 4.3%, a
market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 0.92, we calculate
Chipbond's WACC as 10.2%. Our model assumes 2% long-term revenue
growth starting in 2036E and EBIT margin of 18.6% in 2028E with GM
expansion in the following years. Our target price is equivalent to a P/E of

41x/28x 2026/27E and P/B of 3.4/3.2x 2026/27E.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) lower-than-expected photonics contribution; 2) lower-than-
expected smartphone sales, especially for OLED smartphone models; 3)
lower-than-expected PCNB demand or 4K TV penetration, and 4) failure to
raise price to at least pass through the inflation.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Catalyst Watch on Hon Precision (7769.TW)
Direction: Upside
Duration: Within 90 Days (expires 27 Aug 2026)
Date Added: 28 May 2026
Catalyst: Thematic driven
We are constructive on Hon Precision's earnings growth outlook driven by its solid engagement in global leading AI
projects. For 2Q earnings, our estimates are higher than consensus as we expect higher margins from a better sales mix
and operating leverage, which we believe could drive strong >100% YoY earnings growth. Also, though a long-term

opportunity, we believe co-packaged optics (CPO) engagement with key customers could also drive significant upside
potential.

### Page 7

Hon Precision (7769.TW)
31 July 2026 Citi Research
7
Hon Precision
Company description
Hon Precision is a leading Taiwanese manufacturer specializing in IC
semiconductor automation equipment. Headquartered in Taichung City,
Taiwan, it is most known globally for providing high-precision IC test
handlers, temperature control solutions, and complete automated integrated
solutions for major OSAT and IDM customers.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Hon Precision as we view it sitting in a structurally

protected position at the heart of the AI/HPC back-end test cycle, with
growth visibility extending well into the next few years — the company holds
>90% share of the AI/HPC FT handler market and 60-70% of the ASIC SLT
market. We forecast a solid strong sales revenue CAGR in 2025-28E with
gross margin continuing to expand. In our view, the binding constraint on Hon
Precision's near-term trajectory is now capacity, rather than demand —
placing the company in the early phase of a multi-year structural growth and

margin-expansion cycle.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$11,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 36x applied
to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates. Our PER target of 35x is set
at 1-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC
testing handler market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR
of 77% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in 2022-25. Our
estimated 77% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average

(Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing equipment peers. As its peers
have been trading at  average  36x PER on 2027 /28 consensus EPS in the past
1 year, we believe Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target PER
of 36x.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price
volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we are not assigning a High
Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of > 75%, significantly stronger

than the growth outlook for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant
position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker capacity build for
semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI
projects; 3) slower-than-expected capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the

company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-
expected development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing
time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak adoption of high-
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Realtek (2379.TW)
30 July 2026 Citi Research
2
Realtek
Valuation
Our target price for Realtek is set at NT$630 based on our target PE multiple of 17x to our 2026-2027 EPS average. We believe
our PER of 17x, in line with its 3-year forward PER average, is justified by its solid growth opportunity in automotive and
switch/PON business. We also believe its continued efforts in products mix optimization should help to support its margins
expansion. At our target price, the shares would trade at a 2026/27E P/B of 5x/4x.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) Slowdown in technology migration for its networking business; 2) Weaker-
than-expected growth momentum for automotive Ethernet sales; 3) Worse-than-expected PC/NB and consumer electronic
demand; 4) Slower-than-expected GM expansion due to pricing competition from peers; and 5) Higher-than-expected
memory cost burden to dampen the order outlook from its networking and PC/NB clients.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Key takeaways from Delta’s results call today (July 30th) include: 
(1) Delta revised up its CAPEX investment in 2026 to be NT$70bn 
(previous guidance was expected to be 10%+ higher than 2025, 
implying NT$50bn+). The company also expects CAPEX to remain at 
a similar or higher level in 2027 if industry growth remains strong. 
Delta mentioned that its capacity expansion investments are 
planned on a 2+ year forward-looking basis (was 1+ year previously) 
and is currently expanding in multiple regions (including Thailand, 
mainland China, US, Taiwan, etc). 
(2) Management expects 2H26 revenue to outperform 1H26 mainly 
due to the ramp up of new products and increasing contribution 
from projects with CSP customers. Management mentioned that 
achieving a GM of ~35% in 2H26 is a reasonable target, but the 
actual level will remain subject to the product mix. However, we 
believe the 2Q26 GM of 35.6% was seeing a negative impact from 
inventory write-offs (~1.0ppt negative impact) and we continue to 
see GM to be driven by higher AI exposure going forward (2026 GM 
at 37.0% per GSe). 
(3) For HVDC products, the company believes shipment for both 
+-400VDC and 800VDC solutions will start in 3Q26, and expects 
more contribution in 2027. The +-400VDC solution is expected to 
see more shipment as Delta mentioned that some CSP clients are 
more aggressive in adopting this solution while the 800VDC solution 
is mainly driven by Nvidia’s architecture. 
(4) In terms of new products, Delta has already shipped the SOFC 
solution to a couple of customers for testing, and expects to have a 
trail production line ready by the end of 2026 and complete a new 
production line in 2027. As for SST, Delta mentioned the solution is 
being adopted by some clients, mainly for smaller scale data 
centers, and expects that commercialization still takes time.

### Page 3

(5) In terms of supply chain risk, the company continues to see tight supply conditions
across various materials but is still able to secure supply given its strong supplier
relationships. Pricing adjustment to reflect higher costs will require discussion with
clients and the ability to pass costs on to customers is still dependent on the products.
(6) The company expects AI to contribute 25%+ of total revenue and expects liquid
cooling related products to account for 12%+ of total revenue in 2026.

Investment view
We maintain our positive view on Delta’s revenue/profitability outlook in the coming
years (see here), and we expect GM/OPM will further increase by 10ppt+/14ppt+ in
2028 vs. 2025, due to the increasing contribution from AI power products (expand from
9% in 2025 to 69% in 2028E), whose margin profile is much higher than Delta’s
corporate average. In order to meet with the strong AI power demand, the company has
revised up its 2026 CAPEX guidance to reach NT$70bn (vs. previous guidance of

~NT$50bn) and continues to expand capacity in multiple regions. With new the 12kW
PSU to ramp up from 2Q26 and more contribution from HVDC power racks starting in
3Q26, we expect 2Q26 GM of 35.6% to mark the low point in 2026-28E, followed by
sequential margin expansion through 2028E.
Moreover, following management’s comments from the latest results call, we see
meaningful revenue upside from the adoption of the HVDC solution, which is expected
to start in 3Q26, with dollar content per watt to be 3x+ more than the company’s

current key PSU products. Moreover, the company continues to develop next-gen SST
solution for future CSPs’ HVDC datacenters, which could deliver 10x+ dollar content per
watt than existing products. For HVDC power rack and SST solutions, we expect Delta to
capture a leading market share in both markets (50-70%+ in 2027E-2030E), supported
by its industry leading AC to DC conversion capabilities. These offerings are likely to
become key revenue and profit drivers for the company for at least the next five years.

Overall, we maintain our positive view on Delta, and expect the company to continue
delivering solid earnings growth in the long term, considering the company’s leading
position in the most critical segment of the AI datacenter power industry. Maintain our
Buy rating on Delta, and lower our 12m TP from NT$4,500 to NT$4,120.
2Q26 earnings recap
Delta’s 2Q26 core business (OPI) was 21/9% lower than both GSe/BBG consensus while
2Q26 GM miss GSe/BBG consensus by 3.1/0.8ppt as GM performance normalized

without penalty income that occurred in 1Q26 (which lifted GM by ~0.7ppt). Moreover,
2Q26 GM was partially offset by a ~1.0ppt impact from an inventory write-off. On
earnings, Delta’s 2Q net income was 13% lower than GSe due to the lower contribution
from Delta Thailand.

due to the potential shortage in materials (we revise down revenue estimates by3/4/4% 
for 2026/27/28E).

based on an unchanged 26.5x P/E multiple (+1.6x STDV higher than Delta’s past 15-year

average valuation), and maintain our valuation period at 2028E and discounted back to

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Delta Electronics is the largest vendor of power supply components globally (in terms of 
sales) and a key servo motor/inverter/PLC supplier in mainland China. It is also a key 
supplier for the EV/server power industry, holding one of the largest market shares in 
both markets. We are positive on Delta’s long-term growth opportunity driven by 
multiple factors (EV/EV charger/5G infrastructure/Datacenter power/ESS etc.) in the 
coming years. We also expect demand for its AI server power and cooling systems to 
deliver solid growth given the increasing demand for high computing performance AI 
servers, which will require higher efficiency power components and better cooling 
systems to improve the overall performance. Trading at a 1-year forward P/E multiple 
that is below our target multiple, we believe the stock is undervalued, given the 
company’s strong revenue outlook (50%+ 2025-28E CAGR) for its growth sectors 
(including EV & EV charging / green capex & renewable energy / cloud computing / 
smart manufacturing / satellite communication & smart connectivity), and its leadership 
in the power component industry. We are Buy rated. Key downside risks include: (1) 
slower-than-expected AI server power consumption growth momentum, (2) potential 
market share loss risk in AI server DC-DC power system or the potential design change 
on AI server DC-DC system, and (3) slower than expected of future products 
deployment, including power rack, SST and SOFC. 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Delta. Our 12m TP of NT$4,120 is based 
on a target P/E multiple of 26.5x (1.6x s.d. higher than Delta’s past 15-year average 
valuation) applied to our 2028E EPS and discounted back to 2027 at a CoE of 11%. 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected AI server power consumption growth 
momentum, (2) potential market share loss risk in AI server DC-DC power system or the 
potential design change on AI server DC-DC system, and (3) slower than expected of 
future products deployment, including power rack, SST and SOFC.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 1

Idea
2027 LEAP revenue guidance raised from US$5.5bn to at least US$7.5bn; we now
forecast US$8.5bn: On the July 30 earnings call, management said that 2026 LEAP
(leading-edge advanced packaging and testing) is tracking ahead and raised guidance
from US$3.5bn to US$3.7bn. Management also lifted the 2027 LEAP target from US
$5.5bn to at least US$7.5bn, a strong beat vs. both our estimate and the buy-side
bogey of ~US$6bn. We attribute this to strong AI-related demand, including strong

server CPU, which has shown meaningful development over the past quarter. We
now expect LEAP revenue of US$3.7bn in 2026 and US$8.5bn in 2027, mostly
driven by strong CPU (full process) and ASIC/GPU demand (oS). See Exhibit 4 for
our detailed LEAP revenue breakdown.
Another rise in 2026e capex; 2027e capex could remain high: In our prior report
ASE: Strong AI customer demand to trigger price hike, we noted that Taiwan's OSAT
capacity is full and that ASE and KYEC are devoting record-high capex to expand. As

expected, the company raised 2026e capex again from US$8.5bn to US$10.5bn. It is
simultaneously building 13 greenfield and seven brownfield sites. Management
indicated that 2027e capex could remain high, suggesting extremely strong demand
in the following years and limited concern about potential demand disruption from
Intel EMIB. This also echoes TSMC’s latest earnings call, in which it welcomed Intel’s
role in helping alleviate advanced packaging supply constraints.

Gross margin continues to expand: ASE’s ATM margin expansion is ahead of our
expectation. The company said it is likely to exceed the high end of its structural
range (30%) by 4Q26 and expects margins to keep rising sequentially in 2027. We
attribute the expansion to higher LEAP exposure, price hikes, and higher utilization
from legacy process as well.
Raise price target to NT$628; stay OW: We view the implied target P/E of 21x for
2027e as attractive given the strong >70% earnings CAGR from 2025–2028e.

We see even more upside from LEAP in 2027
ASE reported 2Q26 margin of 21.0%, up 1.0ppts Q/Q, in line with both our estimate and
consensus. The margin expansion stemmed mostly from higher utilization rate with
greater operating efficiency.   Looking into 3Q26, the company guided for revenue to be up
21-22% with gross margin to stay flat Q/Q given higher EMS business growth from
memory price hike. The company expects ATM margin to exceed the ceiling of the

structural range (30%) by 4Q26 and continues to expand sequentially in 2027.

We now forecast  ASE's LEAP revenue at US$3.7bn in 2026
and US$8.5bn in 2027
In our prior report, ASE Technology Holding Co. Ltd.: AI/CPO outlook exceeds our
aggressive assumptions; OW (30 Apr 2026), our bottom-up analysis suggested that  ASE's
LEAP (leading-edge advanced packing) revenue is likely to reach US$3.5bn in 2026 and US

$6bn in 2027, given strong demand from both oS (on substrate) outsourcing and CPU
CoWoS needs. However,  ASE management hinted that 2027 LEAP revenue could be more
than double new 2026e guidance of US$3.7bn, suggesting that 2027e LEAP revenue could
reach US$7.5bn or above. At the same time, we have also been seeing strong momentum
of AI-related demand over the past quarter, including but not limited to very strong server
CPU demand –  both Venice and Vera CPU could grow more than double in 2027, in our

view. We therefore update our LEAP revenue forecast below – our new assumptions are
US$3.7bn in 2026 and US$8.5bn in 2027.
Below are the assumptions in our bottom-up analysis, showing how we come up with
our US$8.5bn forecast.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
The LEAP demand is mostly from oS  outsourcing from TSMC. While ASE remains the
major oS supplier to Nvidia GPU, our checks also indicate that the majority of Google TPU
and AMD MI400 series is likely to be outsourced to ASE given TSMC's clean room
limitations. The dollar content per chip for oS at ASE could range from US$100 to US
$200 in 2026, depending on the material cost, chip size, and margin of each product.  Our
current assumption of the oS outsourcing percentage from TSMC of major products is –

Nvidia almost 100%, TPU 70-90%, MI400 80%.
Meanwhile, we expect that AMD's Venice CPU could go for full CoWoS-like packaging at
ASE group (both ASE and SPIL). The initial allocation could be more to SPIL, given SPIL's
better production capability. The allocation could eventually reach 50:50 between SPIL
and ASE. By assuming US$8k per CoWoS wafer cost at ASE and that the chip per CoWoS
wafer could be around 25-26 chips for Venice CPU, we expect the dollar content per

Venice CPU to be slightly above US$300.
Besides the major products mentioned above, we also expect Vera CPU and other AI ASIC
chips (Microsoft Maia, Meta MTIA, etc.) to go through either oS or full CoWoS-like
process at ASE, while Vera could now show >2x volume in 2027, from our industry checks.

AI semi packaging updates:  non-TSMC CoWoS capacity
growing rapidly

TSMC's tight capacity. Aside from the existing oS outsourced from TSMC for NVIDIA GPU,
we are quite positive on demand for ASEH CoWoS, and we expect its CoWoS capacity to
double in 2026.

Valuation methodology
We lift  our price target from NT$558 to NT$628: This reflects our earnings estimate
revisions for 2026-28.
We continue to derive our price target using the base case value from a residual income
model. All of the key assumptions are unchanged, including cost of equity of 9.8% (beta
of 1.3, risk premium of 6% and risk-free rate of 2%),  intermediate growth rate of 9%, and
terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Our bull case value rises from   NT$655 to NT$700, while our bear case  value increases

from  NT$355 to NT$380, implying 23x and 13x our 2027e EPS, respectively

Relative valuation
Over the past two months, the share price has rallied a lot given investors' expectations of
CPO testing and margin expansion. However, the stock is still trading at 16x our 2027e
EPS, which we view as attractive for a company that has increasing pricing power, AI
revenue, and margin growth. Our price target implies 21x our 2027e EPS, close to   +2SD
from its historical average of 13x since 2015.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 091: 260730_ms_AUO

### Page 1

M
Update
AU Optronics  | Asia Pacific
New Applications for Glass to
Take Longer
We don't see any strong catalysts from here with a lukewarm
outlook for its core business and limited contributions from new
applications. Stay EW.
3Q26 guidance below seasonal: Management guided for display revenue (48% of
2Q26 mix) to decline modestly QoQ as consumer electronics demand should be
relatively  soft amid elevated component costs and earlier restocking in 1H26.

Mobility revenue (29% of 2Q26 mix) should remain roughly flat  QoQ as Europe
enters the slow season and China sees weakening demand. Vertical revenue (19% of
2Q26 mix) is expected to rise by low single digits % QoQ as orders from commercial
displays and green solutions remain stable. We view 3Q26 guidance as below
seasonal, although the revenue mix might turn a bit more favorable since the
mobility and vertical business generated  higher operating margins of 3.8% and 5.6%,

respectively, vs. display at -3.2% in 2Q26. For the longer term, it maintains its target
to have 70% of revenue from mobility and vertical solutions by 2030 (vs. 43% in
2025).
New business initiatives to  take time: For glass in advanced packaging, AUO has
been developing the TGV and RDL process for FOPLP and glass core. It is engaging
with potential customers for LEO antenna modules and micro LED optical modules,
which we think might see only limited revenue contribution in the coming years.

Similar to its display supply chain peers, it is looking into the glass core substrate
space and thinks it might take another 2-3 years for the process to mature. Mini/
micro LED is still on AUO's agenda for wearables and automobile applications.
though we think the complexity in its process remains a big hurdle for this ultimate
display technology to be commercialized in mainstream use cases. For CPO, it is in
the designing and sampling process with one optical interconnect supplier, although

we think it will take more time for AUO's integrated solution (micro LED +
photodiode + glass substrate) to be adopted as the industry is still trying to sort out
all of the issues in the current architecture.
More asset disposals: AUO announced plans to sell its Kaohsiung and Huaya fabs
for expected gains of NT$4.28bn and NT$13.39bn, respectively; timing for
recognition has not been finalized.
Stay EW: With a lackluster outlook for its core business and emerging businesses

Results Summary
Earnings turnaround in 2Q26: Off  revenue of NT$70,891mn (+3% QoQ/+2% YoY), gross
margin expanded by 1.2ppt to  13% on a more favorable product mix and improving
operating efficiency. With the opex ratio flat QoQ at 12.7%, operating profit was NT
$218mn, slightly better than MSe of a   NT$153mn loss but in line with consensus of profit
NT$238mn.
With a non-op gain of NT$2,533mn (mainly from asset disposal gain of ~NT$2.3bn), net

income came in at NT$1,343mn (EPS NT$0.18), surpassing  our expectation of NT$65mn
(EPS NT$0.01)  and consensus of NT$356mn (EPS NT$0.05).

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
AU Optronics (2409.TW)
Base case, 2026e P/B of 1.4x. We believe price/book value better reflects AUO's intrinsic value,
in view of the industry's relatively cyclical nature. The stock's mid-cycle average has been 0.8x
P/B, but it has re-rated due to its CPO and LEO businesses. We see our target multiple as
reasonable in consideration of the company's business diversification efforts, although they

will take time to contribute.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected end-demand
n Disciplined capacity control among peers
n Favorable panel pricing environment
n Faster progress on new business initiatives
Risks to Downside
n Macro headwinds leading to weak demand
n Faster yield improvement among Chinese players, leading to more supply
n Faster OLED panel adoption among large-size applications
n Slower progress on new business initiatives

buy recommendation; we correspond Equal-weight and Not-Rated to hold and Underweight to sell recommendations, respectively.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 105: 260731_daiwa_umc

Source PDF: all report pdf/260731_daiwa_umc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-07-31
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo, we lift our forecasts and TP for 
UMC to factor in its stronger-than-expected wafer shipment on AI and 
automotive & industrial (A&I) strength, followed by order reallocation as a 
result of the foundry capacity consolidation. We expect its price hike to go 
through for extra margin accretion and earnings growth, premised upon its 
optimal utilisation. That said, we remain comfortable with our Hold (3) call 
on the stock as we believe the volume and ASP strength is already in the 
price, given the equity market’s hefty expectation over 2027-28. TSMC 
(2330 TT, TWD2,205, Buy [1]) is our preferred foundry exposure.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 
10-16% for UMC on 2 reasons: stronger-than-expected wafer shipment and 
price hike to go through effective in 2027. Thanks to strong demand for AI 
companion chips such as power management, FPGA (field-programmable 
gate array) and connectivity including new ramp of SiPh (silicon-photonics) 
based on 12” wafers for competitiveness, as well as robust demand for A&I 
chips more than enough to offset consumer weakness, we expect UMC’s 
capacity utilisation rate (CUR) to reach 91% in 3Q26. We further forecast 
the CUR to reach 100% in 2H27, thanks to extra demand from customers’ 
order reallocation as a result of the foundry capacity consolidation, 
including PSMC’s (6770 TT, not rated) fab P5 sale and TSMC’s mature 
capacity optimisation. Of note, we hike our 2026E EPS by 60% which is 
inflated by UMC’s hefty non-operating gains of TWD30bn in 2Q26 from 
financial assets marked-to-market and investment gains.

Price hike still likely. Management expects some uplift in wafer prices for 
2027; we concur since given our projected CUR ramping trajectory, we still 
see a good chance for the price hike to go through, so long as the AI 
buildout cycle sustains to drive favourable supply/demand dynamics amid 
the foundry capacity consolidation. We are modelling 2-5% hikes effective 
from 1Q27 across selected process nodes especially related to the AI and 
A&I applications, which should continue to drive UMC’s 28nm-and-below 
ramp to 45% of revenue (37% in 2Q26) for blended ASP improvement.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lift our 12M TP for 
UMC to TWD108 (previous: TWD71), based on our higher ROE-adjusted 
PBR target of 3.3x (previous: 2.4x) on 4-quarter-forward BVPS due to 
higher ROE trajectory. Yet, we keep our Hold (3) rating on the stock as we 
believe its volume and ASP story already in the price. Key upside/downside 
risks would be any price hikes above our expectation/geopolitical 
overhangs (war, FX, interest rate) weighing on end-demand.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 14-19% below the consensus 
forecasts as we believe the street is too bullish on UMC’s pricing power.

31 July 2026

Price hike to go through? Still likely

➢ Increasing forecasts on favourable wafer loading and pricing 
➢ Price hike likely to go through on improving supply/demand  
➢ Maintaining Hold (3) on valuation grounds; higher 12M TP of TWD108

Source: Daiwa forecasts

Company profile 
Incorporated in Taiwan, United Microelectronics Corp (UMC) is one of the leading semiconductor 
foundries in the world and second largest in Taiwan. UMC offers a series of foundry services from 
photo-mask, wafer fabrication to wafer probe & test. Its wafer fabs are spread over Taiwan, China and 
Singapore, with technology offerings ranging from 0.25u and above, 0.18/0.15u, 0.13u, 90nm, 65nm, 
45/40nm, 28/22nm to 14nm FF.

the amount of the transaction will be in excess of the required collateral or margin requirements**.

estate prices, commodity prices, and others. In addition, depending on the content of the transaction, the loss could exceed the amount of the collateral or margin requirements.  
• There may be a difference between bid price etc. and ask price etc. of OTC derivatives handled by our company.  
• Before engaging in any trading, please thoroughly confirm accounting and tax treatments regarding your trading in financial instruments with such experts as certified public accountants.

### Page 1

M
Update
Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Our Thoughts on the Share
Price Movement
Chroma's share price was down 6.7% (limit-down during the session) vs.  the
TAIEX up ~1%.
We think the underperformance was mainly driven by two investor concerns:
• Rubin Ultra might only have a TDP of  1,800W as the mainstream spec, which
might affect its GPU SL T growth in 2027.
• Chroma might not win CPU SL T orders from one major chip vendor.
Our views:
• We believe that 1,800W TDP for the Rubin Ultra GPU does not sound

reasonable. The TDP for the Rubin GPU already reaches 1,800-2,300W. Our
checks suggest that the mainstream spec for Rubin Ultra should be higher
than that of Rubin and will need a higher spec of SL T from Chroma as well.
• For the SL T business, Chroma used to focus more on the GPU market, but it
is turning  more aggressive on  CPUs  as well. It has already secured the CPU
SL T project with one hyperscaler and is qualifying with a major chip vendor

for its new CPU model.
• We stay OW on Chroma (price target NT$2,800, 45x 2027e P/E) and would
suggest building positions into the dip for the growth potential driven by
power, semiconductor, and photonics testing tools.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

順達(3211) 
雖 Q2 營運逆風，然 BBU 需求強勁，全年展望不變 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $360 
前次投資建議：無

報告日期：2026.07.30 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ BBU出貨遞延，Q2營運不如預期，EPS 1.4 元。 
➢ 高瓦數 BBU有望於 H2 開始出貨，全年展望不變，2026 年 EPS 9.8元。 
➢ HVDC與高壓 BBU 預計於 2027 量產，產品組合續朝高瓦數升級，EPS 18.0元，兆豐投顧
給予目標價 360 元(20X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 16.9%，投資評等為「逢低買進」。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/7/29 收盤價： 308.00 ➢ 順達 (3211)公告 26Q2財報：營收 34.9億元， QoQ 
+4.4%/YoY +7.5% ，低於市場共識，主要受到 BBU客
戶拉貨遞延影響所致，在整體產品組合轉差下，毛利率
下滑至13.9%，季減6.1ppt，故稅後淨利2.07億元，QoQ 
-33.8%/YoY -65.1%，EPS 1.4元，低於市場預期。 
➢ 展望2026年，雖Q2客戶拉貨遞延帶動營運短期逆風，
不過客戶拉貨已恢復正常，同時隨AI資料中心備援電池
需求強勁且8KW、12KW高瓦數機種預計於 H2開始放
量，隨瓦數與電芯數增加帶動單價上揚，營運動能可望
回升，兆豐投顧預估順達2026年營收149.5億元，YoY 
+13.1%，在非IT（BBU）營收佔比可望超過整體營收
50%下，預估毛利率18.9%，年增2.3ppt，稅後淨利15.1
億元，YoY +9.2%，EPS 9.8元。 
➢ 展望 2027年，在電源架構升級下 BBU已然成為必需
品，且AI server供耗持續提升帶動更高壓與容量之BBU
需求持續增長，故順達持續於台灣與泰國擴充數倍產
能，同時公司亦持續投入研發25KW以上高瓦數BBU及
800V HVDC產品配合客戶開發送樣，並有望於2027年
開始量產，兆豐投顧預估順達2027年營收186.9億元，

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順達轉型加速，AI server BBU營收可望於 2026年佔比過半  
順達(3211)成立於 1998 年並於 2004 年掛牌上市，以筆記型電腦及行動裝置
鋰電池模組起家，公司於中國蘇州吳江、昆山及泰國大城皆有建立生產據點。
近年公司積極進行業務轉型，從傳統NB/平板電池模組跨入 AI伺服器備援電
池(BBU)、電動輔助自行車 (E-Mobility)及儲能系統 (ESS)等高毛利非 IT 應
用，目前產品涵蓋 NB/平板、伺服器 BBU、電動車用電池組及儲能模組，終
端應用遍及筆電品牌大廠、電源供應器大廠及 CSP資料中心客戶。順達原為
國內 NB 電池模組主力供應商，目前已成功將營運重心轉向 AI 伺服器 BBU
領域，非IT(以 BBU為主)營收佔比可望於 2026 年首度超車傳統 IT 業務，躍
升為公司最大營收貢獻來源，2025~2026 年持續擴充 BBU產能並升級規格，
也規劃規劃更高功率 HVDC 架構產品，全面迎接 AI 資料中心機櫃功耗提升
所帶動的備援電力需求。

- 3 - 
個股報告

BBU 出貨遞延，Q2營運不如預期，EPS 1.4元 
順達(3211)公告 26Q2 財報：營收 34.9 億元，QoQ +4.4%/YoY +7.5%，低
於市場共識，主要受到 BBU客戶拉貨遞延影響所致，在整體產品組合轉差
下，毛利率下滑至 13.9%，季減 6.1ppt，故稅後淨利 2.07 億元，QoQ 
-33.8%/YoY -65.1%，EPS 1.4元，低於市場預期。

BBU 拉貨重啟，非 IT 營收比重續增，獲利動能回升，EPS 2.8元 
兆豐投顧預估順達 26Q3 營收 38億元，QoQ +8.9%/YoY +5.0%，主要受惠
BBU機種客戶自 7月恢復正常拉貨，同時 AI 資料中心備援電池需求強勁，
預期非 IT 營收佔比持續提升，帶動整體營收回升；在產品組合改善下，毛利
率回升至 20.0%，季增 6.1ppt，稅後淨利 4.4 億元，QoQ +111.9%/YoY 
+83.9%，EPS 2.8元。

高瓦數 BBU有望於 H2開始出貨，全年展望不變，2026年 EPS 9.8元 
展望 2026 年，雖 Q2客戶拉貨遞延帶動營運短期逆風，不過客戶拉貨已恢復
正常，同時隨 AI資料中心備援電池需求強勁且 8KW、12KW高瓦數機種預
計於 H2開始放量，隨瓦數與電芯數增加帶動單價上揚，營運動能可望回升，
兆豐投顧預估順達 2026 年營收 149.5 億元，YoY +13.1%，在非 IT（BBU）
營收佔比可望超過整體營收 50%下，預估毛利率 18.9%，年增 2.3ppt，稅後
淨利 15.1 億元，YoY +9.2%，EPS 9.8元。

HVDC與高壓 BBU 預計於 2027量產，產品組合續朝高瓦數升級，EPS 18.0
元 
展望 2027 年，在電源架構升級下 BBU已然成為必需品，且 AI server 供耗
持續提升帶動更高壓與容量之 BBU需求持續增長，故順達持續於台灣與泰國
擴充數倍產能，同時公司亦持續投入研發 25KW以上高瓦數 BBU 及 800V 
HVDC產品配合客戶開發送樣，並有望於 2027 年開始量產，兆豐投顧預估
順達 2027 年營收 186.9 億元，YoY +25.0%，隨高瓦數產品比重提升，毛利
率可望維持在 20%以上，整體獲利顯著擴大，預估稅後淨利27.8 億元，YoY 
+84.2%，EPS 18.0元。

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內 容必須經本公司同意。

## Document 129: 3443創意 統一

Source PDF: all report pdf/3443創意 統一.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-03
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  1

2026/08/03 投資速報

電子–半導體 
創意 (3443.TT) 
產能爭取無虞，迎接 26-28 年爆發成長期

強力買進(調升評等) 
出刊緣由：財報公佈 
與前次比較： 上次 本次 
評 等 買進 強力買進 
目標價 5070 6080

市值(億) 5098.7 
股本(億) 13.400000

淨負債/股東權益 177.64 
董監持股比率 34.84 
外資持股比率 25.70 
投信持股比率 10.18 
個股與大盤走勢

近一個月 近三個月 近半年 近一年 
加權指數報酬率 -6.5 10.8 34.5 83.2 
個股股價報酬率 -21.5 -10.3 45.9 214.6

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部對創意的投資建議調升至強力買進，目標價上修至6080 元，
係因(1)2Q26 財報優於市場預期，(2)3Q26 動能持續上修，(3)產能
取得順利，CPU 領域有產能的廠商將受惠，加上下一代產品明年將
開始量產，故貢獻程度有所上修，(4)車用晶片雖然北美客戶 27 年
貢獻程度下修 ， 不過中國客戶補上 ，28 年展望更為樂觀 。 綜合以上 ，
上修創意 26/27 年 EPS 分別為 54.69 元(原估 52.55 元，+94.4%YOY)
及 152.4 元(原估 112.5 元，+178.8%YOY)。近期股價隨大盤大幅修
正，不過時序進入26 年下半年，創意的成長動能更加明確上修，且
費用控管十分優異，客戶從雲端 AI 延伸至終端 AI，未來更有光通
高速傳輸潛在客戶，故對創意看法更加樂觀，調升投資建議至強力
買進，目標價調升至 6080 元(2027 年EPS*40XPER)。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q26 獲利大幅優於預期：  
2Q26 創 意 營 收 為 大 幅 優 於 預 期 的138.9 億 元 ， 成 長
21.4%QOQ，成長 127.6%YOY，其中，量產營收 115.8 億元，
成長 18%QOQ，成長188%YOY，NRE 及 IP 營收 23.1 億元，成
長 41%QOQ，成長 11%YOY，量產及營收皆大幅優於預期，量
產主要原因是在晶圓代工廠爭取展能順利 ， 使得出貨量增長
比預期各樂觀；而在 NRE 方面，也是數個客戶產品順利
SHIPOUT 所致。但毛利率下滑至 21.55%，不如原先預期的
24%，係因 SHIPOUT 的 NRE 有包含光罩及晶圓成本，加上量
產也比預期高所致 。 費用方面控管良好 ， 僅有RD 費用增加 ，
其餘皆下滑，故營業利益達到 16.7 億元，優於原先預期的
15.8 億元，稅後淨利達到15.54 億元，衰退5.5%QOQ，成長
99%YOY，EPS 11.61 元(原估 10.31 元)。

2. 3Q26 預估上修且續創新高  
展望 3Q26，雖然創意在此次法說會未給財測，不過因為先進
製程產能取得順利，故預估量產營收仍有雙位數成長，然而
NRE 推估部分在 2Q26 提前認列，故將會衰退。另外毛利率也
因為量產持續提升而下滑，不過費用將持續控管在相對低的
水位 。 綜合以上 ， 預估創意3Q26 營收為 159.2 億元(原估 154
億元)， 成長14.6%QOQ， 成長84.8%YOY， 毛利率預估為19%(原
估 22%)，不過費用控管良好，故預估稅後淨利將到15.97 億
元，成長2.7%QOQ，成長84.4%YOY，EPS 11.92 元(原估 12.9
元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. CPU產品持續上修預估： 
研究部先前預估26年 CPU出貨量為200萬顆，但先前預估一
顆晶片為SINGLE DIE ，不過後經訪查後設計為將兩顆DIE封
裝成為一顆晶片，故換算成晶片將為 100萬顆晶片。展望26
年，目前因為X86的CPU缺貨中，故在法說會中公司提到客戶
更願意改用自研ARM BASE的CPU，故究部預估CPU出貨量上修
至120萬顆以上，貢獻營收預估上修為12億美元。展望27年，
目前CPU與該CSP ASIC配比為1:4， 將逐步提升至1:2， 故以目
前市場預估27年CSP ASIC 出貨量將達到800萬顆以上，故搭
配的CPU將至少有200萬顆(400萬顆DIE)以上。另外創意在
4Q26將有CSP 二代CPU TAPE OUT ，而二代CPU單價將略高於
一代產品 ， 故綜合以上 ， 以200萬顆一代CPU及50萬顆二代CPU
計算，將貢獻創意營收25億美元以上(先前預估為17-18億美
元)。

4. 車用晶片北美雖然下修，但中國補上：  
北美車用客戶所用的晶片雖然在今年四月底已經TAPE OUT，
但大幅量產時間點應該會落在3Q27，相對先前樂觀預估2Q27
約慢一個季度 ， 且市場有傳出先前配置為兩顆5代晶片 ， 但現
在降規為1顆4.1代晶片1顆5代晶片，故市場認為該晶片對於
創意的貢獻性有所打折。然而，在法說會中，董事長表示在
5代晶片方面 ， 創意無論TAPE OUT速度及PPA等皆遠優於對手 。
故研究部認為，若競爭對手研發順利，將不會有4.1代晶片，
所以研究員認為目前5代晶片競爭對手仍有研發瓶頸。而從
晶片應用來看，4代晶片是在21年量產，主要支援FSD功能，
故以功能來看 ，4代晶片要支援完全無人駕駛有其風險 ， 故研
究部認為未來還是會全數導入 5代晶片。因為該款晶片先前
預估27年完整貢獻 ， 目前修正為貢獻約2.5個季度 ， 營收原先
預估貢獻15億美元以上 ， 下修為12億美元 。 至於在6.5代晶片
為2nm製程 ， 與台積電配合研發服務客戶 ， 目前與對手的技術
持續拉大，故對該客戶產品的貢獻延續性非常有信心。另外
在中國客戶方面，該客戶的第 7代ADAS晶片已經導入新車使
用，將在27年逐步成為新款車用標配。研調機構數據顯示，
中國客戶在6代高階智能駕駛晶 片26年上半年出貨達到 300
萬套以上 ， 故推估7代晶片在27年搭載在新車上後 ， 保守預估
出貨量將達到百萬套以上，推估貢獻營收將達到 5億美元以
上，總計車用晶片將貢獻15億美元以上，與原預估相當。

在車用晶片未來推估方面，首先在北美客戶方面，因為北美
客戶會將該晶片用在 CYBERCAB 及 OPTIMUS 上，依照北美客
戶給的理論產能 ，27 年將僅是開端 ，28 年推估貢獻幅度將持
續大幅攀升。

圖二、TESLA 對於 CYBERCAB 及 OPTIMUS 量產規劃

資料來源：TESLA，X，統一研究部整理

另外在中國客戶方面 ， 以該客戶自行公告的資料來看 ，2 代、
3 代及 5 代晶片總共出貨超過 400 萬套，而6 代晶片在 2024
年發表後，25 年導入各品牌車廠，截至 26 年上半年，該客
戶的車用晶片總出貨量達到 1000 萬套以上。而 7 代晶片由
創意操刀設計，將在 27 年開始導入各品牌車廠，以 6 代晶
片銷售成長推估，7 代晶片將在 28 年有超過翻倍成長。

5. 新產品及新 CSP 潛在機會： 
(1)NEW CSP AI ASIC 
首先在新 CSP ASIC 客戶方面 ， 公司雖未明確揭露客戶 ， 不過
研究部認為憑藉公司後段設計能力，將提供 CSP 客戶另一個
降低成本的解決方案，客戶可以自行設計前段後，後段及整
合交付創意，畢竟創意具有 HBM BASE DIE 及CPO 相關的IP
及技術 ， 公司將會極力爭取 。 另外 ， 在合約負債及庫存方面 ，
公司表示有幫客戶備料 HBM 等，研究部認為CSP 客戶下一代
ASIC 都會將記憶體升級成 HBM 4，故創意幫客戶備料有可能
是為客戶下一代產品做研發而準備。從公告的合約負債可以
看出，客戶的 PREPAYMENT 持續創高。

圖三、創意各季存貨及合約負債金額

認為越早進入該領域機會越多。至於 CPO 相關產品有沒有機
會搭著加速器量產一起出，公司表示目前案件仍在初期，持
續與客戶合作研發。

利也同步上修。上修創意 26 年營收為 648.6 億元(原估 594
億元)，成長 90%YOY，稅後淨利同步上修預估為 73.28 億元

隨著短期記憶體供需失衡情形難以緩解，加上HBM排擠效應帶來
標準型記憶體外包訂單，力成2026年營運成長根基極為穩固，在
第二季繳出營收創近年新高的優異成績後 ， 受惠於大晶片FCBGA
放量與原物料成本順利轉嫁，第三季及全年逐季成長趨勢已然確
立。更為關鍵的是，鑒於AI晶片算力提升，封裝面積持續突破光
罩極限，傳統12吋晶圓已逐漸無法滿足經濟效益，採用方形載具
的扇出型面板級封裝(FOPLP)成為突破瓶頸的最佳解決方案 ， 力成
斬獲國際大廠AMD的戰略合作，成功驗證業界首款2.5D面板級
EFB互連技術，將導入下一代「Venice」CPU，為因應強勁的客
戶需求，管理層已將全年資本支出上修至500億元，全力擴展
FOPLP產能與晶兆成(TeraPower)的高階測試設備，預計下半年
完成產品認證後，將於2027年上半年進入大規模量產，同時，公
司亦積極切入光通訊領域，與Broadcom於新加坡成立合資公司
發展先進封裝 ， 並利用自身微凸塊技術切入3D Optical Engine封
裝，預計年底開始小量生產，為2028年的CPO市場提前佈局。
整體而言，力成已成功從傳統記憶體封測廠轉型為具備AI高階封
測能力的領軍企業，未來營運方向明確，先進封裝、高階邏輯測試
與記憶體封測三大引擎將共同推升業績爆發 ， 成功切入FOPLP打
入高階AI封裝領域有助於市場對其本益比的重新定價。

 近期投資評等 PTI力成科技(6239.TT)公司簡介 
力成科技(6239.TT)成立於1997年5月，並於2003年4月上櫃掛牌交
易至今，力成科技擁有完整的半導體後段封測能力，足以提供客戶從晶
圓凸塊(Bumping)、晶圓測試(Wafer Sort)、晶圓級封裝(WLCSP)、銲
線封裝(Wire bond Package) 、覆晶封裝(Flip chip Package) 、系統級
封裝(System in Package)、面板級扇出型封裝(Fanout)、3 維矽穿孔
(3DIC TSV)、模組與系統級組裝(System Assembly)、封裝與系統級測
試(Final Test)等服務。前10大股東持股合計36.86%。國內主要封測廠
同業為日月光投控(3711.TT)、京元電子(2449.TT)、頎邦科技(6147.TT)
等。

記憶體與邏輯封裝雙引擎驅動，第二季營運創近年新高
